Postcondition 5 step (ii)'s ORDINARY, non-recovery, uninterrupted completion of the canonical-truncate write (the MORE-frequently-executed path — every uninterrupted migration run — previously LESS verified than its own crash-recovery heal counterpart, an unsanctioned asymmetry) now receives the SAME post-hoc disk read-back verification already required for sealed-shard writes (Postcondition 6(c), F-C3-P6-002) and the DANGEROUS-window heal write (F-C3-P7-001): after `write_atomic` returns, a FRESH on-disk `(length, SHA-256)` read-back MUST match the Backfill Recovery Manifest's `(final_bytes, final_sha256)` — already durably published in step (i), so no new value is computed — before the migration reports success; a mismatch (EC-013, NEW) fails loud as `E-SHD-013` (NEW error code) rather than silently completing over corrupted content.     change: "S-25.02 cluster-3 fix-burst facet-extension propagation (architect; POLICY 9 vp_index_is_vp_catalog_source_of_truth; per BC-1.18.008 v1.9's own routing note, F-C3-P9-004, BLOCKING): NO new VP allocated — this is a same-burst propagation of ONE facet extension BC-1.18.008 v1.9 (product-owner) made to an EXISTING VP, adjudicating BLOCKING-1 from a fresh-eyes PR-diff reviewer on PR #831 (S-25.02 cluster-3) — the FIRST finding on this BC raised by PR-diff review rather than a LOCAL/cross-vendor adversarial spec pass. VP-124 v1.3→v1.4 (BC-1.18.008 v1.9, NEW schema-location correction [Postcondition 3: `backfill_manifest` promoted to an ordinary `Option<BackfillManifest>` field of `ShardIndex`, never a side-channel TOML table] + NEW Composability clause [Postcondition 3: roll-before-backfill ordering tolerated as a genuine first-ever run, never `AlreadyMigrated`/`E-SHD-011`/`E-SHD-012`] + redefined Invariant 3 idempotency basis [`backfill_manifest.is_some()`, never bare shard-index existence] + NEW EC-014/EC-015, F-C3-P9-004): EXTENDED with a SIXTH integration facet — Manifest schema-location and order-independence invariant. The Backfill Recovery Manifest is an ordinary `Option<BackfillManifest>` field of `ShardIndex` itself, never a side-channel table, and therefore survives EVERY subsequent `ShardIndex` load-modify-store cycle (BC-1.18.006's ongoing per-write roll, both self-heal reconciliation paths) with no special-case preservation code; idempotency ('already migrated') is determined SOLELY by `backfill_manifest.is_some()`, never by bare shard-index-file existence or a non-empty `[[shard]]` array alone — a manifest-less index with real sealed shards (the roll-before-backfill ordering) is correctly treated as NOT YET migrated, and the backfill-split tolerates and appends after such a pre-existing shard set rather than assuming a virgin index.     change: "S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fix-burst facet-extension propagation (architect; POLICY 9 vp_index_is_vp_catalog_source_of_truth; per BC-1.18.008 v1.7's own routing note, F-C3-P7-001): NO new VP allocated — this is a same-burst propagation of ONE facet extension BC-1.18.008 v1.7 (product-owner) made to an EXISTING VP, adjudicating a finding from a FRESH LOCAL adversarial pass on S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 (the pass immediately following the v1.6 cross-vendor burst). VP-124 v1.1→v1.2 (BC-1.18.008 v1.7, NEW Manifest-Authoritative Slice-and-Verify Rule [Postcondition 5] + NEW `E-SHD-012` + extended Postcondition 6(c)/Invariant 4 disk-read-back scope + extended Invariant 3, F-C3-P7-001, adjudicating a fresh-context LOCAL adversarial review): EXTENDED with a FOURTH integration facet — heal slice-verification invariant. At the confirmed DANGEROUS window, the healed content is ALWAYS obtained by slicing the canonical file's current bytes at the Manifest-authoritative offset (`original_bytes - final_bytes`, both fields read from the Backfill Recovery Manifest — never from a summed shard-index `bytes_at_seal`), and that slice is NEVER written unless it independently satisfies `sliced.len() == final_bytes AND sha256(sliced) == final_sha256`; a slice that fails this verification (EC-011) fails loud (`E-SHD-012`, NEW) with no write, and the completed heal's own write additionally receives the SAME post-hoc disk read-back verification Postcondition 6(c)/Invariant 4 already requires for sealed-shard writes. The three pre-existing facets (atomicity-under-interruption; idempotency-against-already-sharded; recovery-confirmation correctness) are UNCHANGED. total_vps UNCHANGED 141 (one facet EXTENSION on an existing VP, no new VP allocated). §Summary + §Proof Method Breakdown UNCHANGED (VP-124 still integration; arithmetic 66+43+10+5+6+11=141 VERIFIED). §Full Index: VP-124 row extended with the heal-slice-verification facet + version-bump note (v1.1→v1.2) + title extended to name the new facet. §Story Anchors: VP-124 row extended to match (new EXTENDED paragraph; source_bc citations gain §PC5(Manifest-Authoritative Slice-and-Verify Rule)/§EC-011). §Traceability UNCHANGED (BC-1.18.008 already cited by VP-123/VP-124; no new unique BC; VPs-with-BC UNCHANGED).     change: "S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fix-burst facet-extension propagation (architect; POLICY 9 vp_index_is_vp_catalog_source_of_truth; per BC-1.18.008 v1.6's own routing note, F-C3-P6-001): NO new VP allocated — this is a same-burst propagation of ONE facet extension BC-1.18.008 v1.6 (product-owner) made to an EXISTING VP, adjudicating a finding from a fresh-context CROSS-VENDOR (OpenAI Codex) adversarial review of S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 — the first cross-vendor pass on this BC after five prior same-vendor (Claude) adversary passes missed the gap. VP-124 v1.0→v1.1 (BC-1.18.008 v1.6, NEW Backfill Recovery Manifest [Postcondition 3] + NEW Recovery-Confirmation Rule [Postcondition 5] + tightened Invariant 3, F-C3-P6-001, adjudicating a fresh-context CROSS-VENDOR adversarial review): EXTENDED with a THIRD integration facet — recovery-confirmation correctness invariant. The SAFE/DANGEROUS/AMBIGUOUS disposition of a re-invocation against an already-published shard-index is determined SOLELY by exact whole-file `(length, SHA-256)` comparison against the Backfill Recovery Manifest's two recorded pairs (`original_bytes`/`original_sha256`, `final_bytes`/`final_sha256`) — NEVER by a structural byte-prefix comparison against a re-concatenation of already-sealed shards, which false-positives on legitimate repeated-prefix content and silently destroys real data (the defect this facet's proof targets). Content where a sealed shard's bytes recur as a genuine prefix of the legitimately-migrated final partition (EC-009) is correctly classified SAFE and left byte-for-byte untouched; an on-disk state matching neither manifest value fails loud (`E-SHD-011`, EC-010) rather than silently overwriting. The two pre-existing facets (atomicity-under-interruption; idempotency-against-already-sharded) are UNCHANGED. total_vps UNCHANGED 141 (one facet EXTENSION on an existing VP, no new VP allocated). §Summary + §Proof Method Breakdown UNCHANGED (VP-124 still integration; arithmetic 66+43+10+5+6+11=141 VERIFIED). §Full Index: VP-124 row extended with the recovery-confirmation facet + version-bump note (v1.0→v1.1) + title extended to name the new facet. §Story Anchors: VP-124 row extended to match (new EXTENDED paragraph; source_bc citations gain §PC5/§EC-009/§EC-010). §Traceability UNCHANGED (BC-1.18.008 already cited by VP-123/VP-124; no new unique BC; VPs-with-BC UNCHANGED). push never attempted; HEAD unchanged; no log entry written → exit 2 Postcondition B) and push-failure test has unreachable/read-only remote (local commit + log append SUCCEED per ADR-026 §F-P10-002 ordering, then push fails → exit 2 Postcondition F; local commit + log retained; retry is push-only) — two mutually-distinct test cases per D-575 VP-082-BATS-SPLIT closure; Postcondition F lifted into formal Property block (v1.7: F-P15-001) — push-failure-after-successful-commit-and-append → exit 2; | [VP-105](VP-105.md) | Next-Advance Gate Blocks Agent Dispatch and git commit/push While Marker Exists, Passes When Absent (BC-1.18.002 §PC1+§PC2+§PC3+§PC4) — TWO hooks-registry entries: Arm 1 (validate-unvalidated-mutation-marker; tool ^Agent$; PreToolUse) returns exit_code=2 (block) when .factory/unvalidated-mutation.marker exists; Arm 2 (validate-unvalidated-mutation-marker-git; tool ^Bash$; command filter matching git commit or git push subcommands) blocks git commit/push Bash dispatches while marker exists; both arms return exit_code=0 (pass) when marker absent; non-matching Bash dispatches (git status, git log, git diff, git fetch, git commit-graph write) and non-Agent/non-Bash tools unaffected; v1.3 algorithm covers SIX dimensions: (a) exact subcommand — commit or push only, git commit-graph write NOT matched (EC-011); (b) complete 7-option arg-taking set: -C, -c, --namespace, --git-dir, --work-tree, --super-prefix, --config-env each consume the following token as argument before subcommand identification (EC-013, EC-014, EC-023); (c) compound splitting on &&, or-operator, ;, pipe, &, newline — any segment returning true causes true (EC-015 status&&commit blocked; EC-019 status&&log not blocked); (d) basename identification — strips leading path components and env VAR=x prefix (EC-017 /usr/bin/git commit blocked; EC-020 cat gitfile not blocked); (e) fail-safe on unrecognized options — unrecognized -flag returns true because subcommand position uncertain (EC-022 --unknown-flag commit blocked); (f) POSIX quote-aware tokenization via shell-words crate (Phase 1b) — quoted git invocations IN SCOPE: git "commit" blocked (EC-024), git 'push' origin blocked (EC-025), g'i't commit blocked (EC-026); EC-027 (command substitution), EC-028 (xargs indirection), EC-029 (dynamic eval) OUT OF SCOPE by design; both gate registrations: on_error="continue" (dispatcher fail-open-on-crash per ADR-039 two-axis model), failure_policy=fail-open; manual rm escape hatch documented. | [VP-108](VP-108.md) | Marker Lifecycle Audited Events — Write and Clear Path Emission Correctness (BC-1.18.001 §PC4; BC-1.18.003 §PC1/PC3/PC4/PC5; ADR-048 §D4 v1.4): REVALIDATED clear emits marker.cleared(clear_mode=REVALIDATED, actor_type=validator, reason=null) via `delete_marker_if_pass`; TTL_EXPIRED clear emits marker.cleared(clear_mode=TTL_EXPIRED, actor_type=deadman, reason=null) via `check_and_clear_expired_marker` (dispatcher-native pre-check, v1.1 retarget) [TTL-loudness per ADR-048 §D4]; OPERATOR_OVERRIDE clear emits marker.cleared(clear_mode=OPERATOR_OVERRIDE, actor_type=operator, reason=non-null) via `reconcile_raw_delete` (dispatcher-native, v1.1 retarget); SUPERSEDED clear (v1.2 new PC5) emits marker.cleared(clear_mode=SUPERSEDED, actor_type=system, reason=non-null) at cross-pair marker overwrite time, before the new marker write completes; crash-path native TTL-allow (block_if_marker PC6) does NOT emit marker.cleared (BC-1.18.003 §EC-014). ALL FOUR clear_mode emissions are dispatcher-native (never WASM-plugin-side) via `HostContext::emit_internal` (v1.2 mechanism-precision correction; not a raw `InternalLog::write`) per ADR-048 §D4 v1.3 — the emit_event host ABI's RESERVED_FIELDS enrichment makes plugin-side emission of a foreign trace_id/plugin_name structurally impossible. Wire format per BC-3.08.001 Events 9-10 (marker.cleared nine mandatory fields incl. SUPERSEDED/system, reason conditional; marker.written six mandatory fields). New PC6 (v1.3): `marker.written` emission correctness — `write_indeterminate_marker` returning `Ok(())` → its caller emits `marker.written` via `emit_marker_written(ctx, &fields)` carrying trace_id/plugin_name/artifact_path/cause/expires_at; `Err(_)` → no emission. New PC7 (v1.3, F-P6-001 regression test): negative control — an unmatched `plugin.indeterminate` with NO corresponding `marker.written` (simulating a PreToolUse fail-closed INDETERMINATE or a PostToolUse write failure) → `reconcile_raw_delete` emits ZERO `marker.cleared(OPERATOR_OVERRIDE)`. PC3's fixture retargeted (v1.3): seeds a `marker.written` line (via `emit_marker_written`) instead of a raw `plugin.indeterminate` JSON line, matching what a real successful write now produces. unit-test (HostContext::new + function call + ctx.drain_events() + InternalEvent assertions, matching host/emit_event.rs's proven pattern). source_bc: BC-1.18.001 §PC4 + BC-1.18.003 §PC1/PC3/PC4/PC5 + BC-3.08.001 Events 9-10 + ADR-048 §D4 v1.4. | [VP-112](VP-112.md) | Rotation Archives the changelog: Sequence Without Data Loss or Duplication (Including Crash-Recovery Idempotency at the Archive Boundary) — the oldest N items are moved verbatim into a per-cycle archive and removed from the source; below-threshold invocation is a no-op; a missing `<cycle-name>` directory is created before the archive is written; reruns and `Check` mode never mutate beyond the intended write. **EXTENDED (v1.1, cluster-4 hardening Obs B, S-25.02 F4):** crash-recovery idempotent-append no-duplicate property — `rotate_changelog_at`'s archive-first/source-second write sequence is crash-idempotent at the archive boundary; after an archive-write-success + source-write-failure double-fault + retry, the overflow items appear in the archive EXACTLY ONCE (byte-level tail-match dedup, tail-anchored, normalized for boundary `\n`); `RotationReport.mutated` remains `true` when the source was rewritten even if the archive write was skipped. v1.0: initial creation (architect; S-15.03 pre-PR spec-package VP registration burst). v1.1: cluster-4 hardening Obs B (formal-verifier; S-25.02 F4) — title extended; bcs gains BC-1.18.009 §INV6 (discharged via shared `rotate_changelog_at`); crash-recovery no-duplicate property added (Property Statement points 7-8, two Proof-Method legs, two harness tests, Source-Contract/Traceability cites for BC-10.13.001 §PC8 + §Invariant 2 + §PC5 no-duplicate sentence). source_bc: BC-10.13.001 §PC5/§PC8/§INV2; BC-1.18.009 §INV6. domain_invariants: []. | postcondition | unit-test | SS-10 | — | draft |

| [VP-123](VP-123.md) | Backfill-Split Content Preservation — Concatenated Shards Reproduce the Original Byte-for-Byte, Every Record Lands in Exactly One Shard, and Every Sealed Shard Satisfies the Per-Shard Cap Bound Unless Flagged Oversized — proptest + golden round-trip: `concat(sealed[seq] + current) == original`; every structural record in exactly one shard; `ceil(bytes/cap)` partitions (lower bound); every sealed shard's `bytes_at_seal <= shard_cap_bytes` UNLESS flagged `oversized_record: true` (EC-002 or the degenerate oversized-preamble case, EC-008), with EC-002/EC-008 asserted as the ONLY exceeding entries. v1.0: initial creation (formal-verifier; S-25.02 F2 VP-extension burst). v1.1: EXTENDED per BC-1.18.008 v1.4 (F-C3-P3-001, S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fresh-context adversarial review) — NEW THIRD facet, per-shard-cap invariant: every sealed shard's `bytes_at_seal <= shard_cap_bytes` unless explicitly flagged `oversized_record: true` in the shard index, enforced as Postcondition 6(c)'s fail-loud hard gate against actual on-disk bytes, never merely implied by the packer having run; §Invariant 4 (NEW) states this as a standing invariant. source_bc: BC-1.18.008 §PC2/§PC6(c)(NEW v1.4)/§INV2/§INV4(NEW v1.4)/§EC-002/§EC-007(NEW v1.4)/§EC-008(NEW v1.4). domain_invariants: []. | [VP-124](VP-124.md) | Backfill-Split Is Atomic Under Interruption, Idempotent Against an Already-Sharded Artifact, Recovery-Confirmation Is Exact-Manifest-Based (Never a Byte-Prefix Heuristic), the DANGEROUS-Window Heal's Slice Is Manifest-Derived and Manifest-Verified Before Every Write, and the Happy-Path Canonical-Truncate Write Receives the SAME Post-Hoc Disk Read-Back Verification, and the Backfill Recovery Manifest Is an Ordinary `ShardIndex` Struct Field (Never a Side-Channel Table) That Survives Every Subsequent Load-Modify-Store Cycle, With Idempotency Determined Solely by Manifest Presence (Schema-Location and Order-Independence Invariant) — fault-injection: a crash at any write step leaves original intact OR split complete (never partial); preservation-check failure aborts fail-loud (E-SHD-003) leaving original untouched; re-run adds zero shards; a re-invocation against an already-published shard-index resolves SAFE/DANGEROUS/AMBIGUOUS SOLELY via exact whole-file `(length, SHA-256)` comparison against the Backfill Recovery Manifest's `original_bytes`/`original_sha256` and `final_bytes`/`final_sha256` pairs — never a structural byte-prefix comparison against a re-concatenation of already-sealed shards; a match against neither pair fails loud (`E-SHD-011`) rather than silently overwriting; at the confirmed DANGEROUS window, the healed content is ALWAYS obtained by slicing the canonical file's current bytes at the Manifest-derived offset `original_bytes - final_bytes` (never from a summed shard-index `bytes_at_seal`), and that slice is NEVER written unless it independently satisfies `sliced.len() == final_bytes AND sha256(sliced) == final_sha256` — a slice that fails this verification (EC-011) fails loud (`E-SHD-012`) with no write, and the completed heal's own write receives the SAME post-hoc disk read-back verification Postcondition 6(c)/Invariant 4 requires for sealed-shard writes; ADDITIONALLY, Postcondition 5 step (ii)'s ORDINARY (non-recovery, first-ever, uninterrupted) canonical-truncate write — not merely its DANGEROUS-window heal counterpart — MUST ALSO receive a fresh post-hoc disk read-back, verified against the SAME Backfill Recovery Manifest `(final_bytes, final_sha256)` pair, before the migration reports success; a mismatch (EC-013) fails loud (`E-SHD-013`, NEW) rather than silently completing over corrupted content. ADDITIONALLY, the Backfill Recovery Manifest is an ordinary `Option<BackfillManifest>` field of `ShardIndex` itself (never a side-channel table), so it survives EVERY subsequent `ShardIndex` load-modify-store cycle (BC-1.18.006's ongoing per-write roll, both self-heal reconciliation paths) with no special-case preservation code; idempotency ("already migrated") is determined SOLELY by `backfill_manifest.is_some()`, never by bare shard-index-file existence or a non-empty `[[shard]]` array alone — a manifest-less index with real sealed shards (the roll-before-backfill ordering) is correctly treated as NOT YET migrated, and the backfill-split tolerates and appends after such a pre-existing shard set rather than assuming a virgin index (EC-014, EC-015). v1.0: initial creation (formal-verifier; S-25.02 F2 VP-extension burst). v1.1: EXTENDED per BC-1.18.008 v1.6 (F-C3-P6-001, S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fresh-context CROSS-VENDOR (OpenAI Codex) adversarial review) — NEW THIRD facet, recovery-confirmation correctness invariant: the SAFE/DANGEROUS/AMBIGUOUS disposition of a re-invocation against an existing shard-index is determined SOLELY by exact whole-file `(length, SHA-256)` comparison against the Backfill Recovery Manifest (Postcondition 3/5), NEVER by a structural byte-prefix comparison against a re-concatenation of already-sealed shards — content where a sealed shard's bytes recur as a genuine prefix of the legitimately-migrated final partition (EC-009) is correctly classified SAFE and left byte-for-byte untouched, and an on-disk state matching neither manifest value fails loud (E-SHD-011, EC-010) rather than silently overwriting; §Invariant 3 (tightened v1.6) now requires this exact-manifest-comparison basis and explicitly forbids the byte-prefix heuristic. v1.2: EXTENDED per BC-1.18.008 v1.7 (F-C3-P7-001, S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fresh-context LOCAL adversarial review, the pass immediately following the v1.6 cross-vendor burst) — NEW FOURTH facet, heal slice-verification invariant: the Manifest-Authoritative Slice-and-Verify Rule (Postcondition 5, NEW) makes the DANGEROUS-window heal's slice offset Manifest-derived (`original_bytes - final_bytes`, removing the prior implementation's dependency on a summed shard-index `bytes_at_seal`) and makes the resulting slice's length+hash a mandatory pre-write gate against `(final_bytes, final_sha256)` — a mismatch (EC-011, NEW) fails loud as `E-SHD-012` (NEW error code) with no write, rather than writing an unverified slice; Postcondition 6(c)/Invariant 4's existing post-hoc disk-read-back discipline for sealed-shard writes is extended to cover the heal's own destructive write. This closes the gap where a prior implementation pass derived the recovery offset by summing shard-index `bytes_at_seal` entries and wrote the result without any verification against the Manifest at all — a corrupted or stale `bytes_at_seal` value would have produced a silent mis-heal (record duplication/truncation). v1.3: EXTENDED per BC-1.18.008 v1.8 (F-C3-P8-002, S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fresh LOCAL adversarial pass, the pass immediately following the v1.7 burst) — NEW FIFTH facet, happy-path canonical-write verification invariant: Postcondition 5 step (ii)'s ORDINARY, non-recovery completion of the canonical-truncate write (the final `write_atomic` that discards the original monolithic file on every UNINTERRUPTED migration run — the more-frequently-executed path, previously LESS verified than its own crash-recovery heal counterpart) now receives the SAME post-hoc disk read-back discipline Postcondition 6(c)/Invariant 4 already require for sealed-shard writes and the F-C3-P7-001 heal-write extension: after `write_atomic` returns, a FRESH read-back of the canonical file's on-disk `(length, SHA-256)` MUST match the Backfill Recovery Manifest's `(final_bytes, final_sha256)` — the SAME pair already durably published in step (i), before this check ever runs, so no new value is computed — BEFORE the migration reports success; a mismatch (EC-013, NEW) fails loud as `E-SHD-013` (NEW error code) rather than silently completing over corrupted content. NEW §Invariant 5 generalizes this as one explicit principle spanning all THREE destructive one-time-migration write sites (sealed-shard write, DANGEROUS-window heal write, happy-path canonical-truncate write): no destructive write is ever trusted on its own return value alone. v1.4: EXTENDED per BC-1.18.008 v1.9 (F-C3-P9-004, BLOCKING, S-25.02 cluster-3, adjudicating BLOCKING-1 from a fresh-eyes PR-diff reviewer on PR #831 — the FIRST finding on this BC raised by PR-diff review rather than a LOCAL/cross-vendor adversarial spec pass) — NEW SIXTH facet, Manifest schema-location and order-independence invariant: the Backfill Recovery Manifest is promoted from a hand-appended side-channel `[backfill_manifest]` TOML table to an ORDINARY `backfill_manifest: Option<BackfillManifest>` field of `ShardIndex` itself (Postcondition 3's schema-location correction), so it now round-trips automatically through every `ShardIndex`-touching write site (BC-1.18.006's ongoing per-write roll `publish_shard_index_update`, `self_heal_resume_from_truncate`, `self_heal_reconcile_missing_index_entries`) as an ordinary consequence of struct serialization, with zero special-case preservation code — closing the LIVE data-loss defect where the prior side-channel table was silently dropped on the very first `ShardIndex` re-serialization after a backfill; Invariant 3's idempotency ("already migrated") determination is REDEFINED from bare shard-index-file/`[[shard]]`-array existence to `backfill_manifest.is_some()` SOLELY, and Postcondition 3 gains a Composability clause requiring the backfill-split, when it finds a manifest-less-but-shard-bearing index (the roll-before-backfill ordering — an ordinary BC-1.18.006 roll ran BEFORE the mandatory one-time backfill ever executed for that artifact), to proceed as a genuine first-ever run rather than mis-classifying it as `AlreadyMigrated` or a corrupted/ambiguous state: `original_bytes`/`original_sha256` are computed from the canonical file's CURRENT on-disk content at invocation time, and the backfill's own newly-sealed shards are appended with `seq` continuing from `existing_max_seq + 1`, never disturbing the pre-existing rolled entries. NEW EC-014 (Manifest survives an intervening BC-1.18.006 roll, pinning the struct-field fix) and NEW EC-015 (roll-before-backfill ordering — backfill tolerates and appends after a pre-existing manifest-less shard set, never `AlreadyMigrated`/`E-SHD-011`/`E-SHD-012`). The five pre-existing facets (atomicity-under-interruption; idempotency-against-already-sharded; recovery-confirmation correctness; heal slice-verification; happy-path canonical-write verification) are UNCHANGED. source_bc: BC-1.18.008 §PC5(Recovery-Confirmation Rule, NEW v1.6; Manifest-Authoritative Slice-and-Verify Rule, NEW v1.7; step (ii) happy-path read-back extension, NEW v1.8)/§PC6(c)(disk-read-back scope extended v1.7; extended again v1.8 to the happy-path write; Summary paragraph naming all three sites, NEW v1.8)/§PC3(schema-location correction, NEW v1.9; Composability clause, NEW v1.9)/§INV3(tightened v1.6; extended v1.7; idempotency basis redefined to Manifest presence, v1.9)/§INV4(disk-read-back scope extended v1.7)/§INV5(NEW v1.8, generalized three-site principle)/§EC-003/§EC-004/§EC-009(NEW v1.6)/§EC-010(NEW v1.6)/§EC-011(NEW v1.7)/§EC-013(NEW v1.8)/§EC-014(NEW v1.9)/§EC-015(NEW v1.9). domain_invariants: []. Fault-injection integration harness, extended with fixture-based SAFE-no-op / DANGEROUS-heal / AMBIGUOUS-fail-loud vectors plus a repeated-prefix fixture family generalizing EC-009. **EXTENDED (S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fresh-context LOCAL adversarial review — the pass immediately following the v1.6 cross-vendor burst — F-C3-P7-001, BC-1.18.008 v1.7):** heal slice-verification invariant — at the confirmed DANGEROUS window, the healed content is ALWAYS obtained by slicing the canonical file's current bytes at the Manifest-authoritative offset (`original_bytes - final_bytes`, never from a summed shard-index `bytes_at_seal`), and that slice is NEVER written unless it independently satisfies `sliced.len() == final_bytes AND sha256(sliced) == final_sha256`; a slice that fails this verification (EC-011) fails loud (`E-SHD-012`, NEW) with no write, and the completed heal's own write additionally receives the SAME post-hoc disk read-back verification Postcondition 6(c)/Invariant 4 requires for sealed-shard writes — this closes the gap where a prior implementation pass derived the offset from a summed, independently-corruptible shard-index field and wrote the result unverified. Fault-injection integration harness, extended with a genuine-slice-passes-and-is-written-and-read-back-verified vector plus an EC-011 corrupted-Manifest-fails-loud vector. **EXTENDED (S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 fresh LOCAL adversarial pass — the pass immediately following the v1.7 burst — F-C3-P8-002, BC-1.18.008 v1.8):** happy-path canonical-write verification invariant — Postcondition 5 step (ii)'s ORDINARY, non-recovery, uninterrupted completion of the canonical-truncate write (the MORE-frequently-executed path, previously less verified than its own crash-recovery heal counterpart — an unsanctioned asymmetry) now receives the SAME post-hoc disk read-back discipline: after `write_atomic` returns, a FRESH on-disk `(length, SHA-256)` read-back MUST match the Backfill Recovery Manifest's `(final_bytes, final_sha256)` — already durably published in step (i) — before the migration reports success; a mismatch (EC-013, NEW) fails loud as `E-SHD-013` (NEW) rather than silently completing over corrupted content. NEW §Invariant 5 generalizes the disk-read-back discipline as one principle spanning all three destructive one-time-migration write sites (sealed-shard / heal / happy-path canonical-truncate). Fault-injection integration harness, extended with the EC-013 simulated-truncated-happy-path-write vector (caught via read-back, `E-SHD-013`) plus a positive-control genuine-write-passes vector. **EXTENDED (S-25.02 cluster-3 fresh-eyes PR-diff review on PR #831, BLOCKING-1, F-C3-P9-004, BC-1.18.008 v1.9 — the FIRST finding on this BC raised by PR-diff review rather than a LOCAL/cross-vendor adversarial spec pass):** Manifest schema-location and order-independence invariant — the Backfill Recovery Manifest is promoted from a hand-appended side-channel `[backfill_manifest]` TOML table to an ORDINARY `backfill_manifest: Option<BackfillManifest>` field of `ShardIndex` itself (Postcondition 3), so it survives EVERY subsequent `ShardIndex` load-modify-store cycle (BC-1.18.006's ongoing per-write roll, both self-heal reconciliation paths) with no special-case preservation code — closing the LIVE data-loss defect where the side-channel table was silently dropped on the first `ShardIndex` re-serialization after a backfill; idempotency ("already migrated") is determined SOLELY by `backfill_manifest.is_some()`, never by bare shard-index-file existence or a non-empty `[[shard]]` array alone (Invariant 3, redefined) — a manifest-less index with real sealed shards (the roll-before-backfill ordering) is correctly treated as NOT YET migrated, and the backfill-split tolerates and appends after such a pre-existing shard set (Postcondition 3's Composability clause: `original_bytes`/`original_sha256` computed from the canonical file's CURRENT content at invocation time; the backfill's own shards appended at `seq` continuing from `existing_max_seq + 1`) rather than mis-classifying it `AlreadyMigrated` or ambiguous/corrupted. Fault-injection integration harness, extended with NEW EC-014 (seal a backfill, THEN trigger an ordinary BC-1.18.006 roll, THEN assert `backfill_manifest` is still present and byte-identical) and NEW EC-015 (seed a manifest-less shard-index via an ordinary roll BEFORE any backfill, invoke the backfill-split, assert it proceeds as a fresh run — never `AlreadyMigrated`, never `E-SHD-011`/`E-SHD-012` — with the pre-existing rolled entry UNCHANGED and the backfill's own entries appended at continuing `seq` numbers). feasible-pending-harness (F4 code). |
